# Feature Inventory

> Every user-visible capability of the product — each page, panel, tab, modal, action and settings
> toggle reachable from `frontend/src/App.tsx`, and each external interface — traced UI → API →
> backend route → service → store, with a status and the evidence for it. The owner rules from this
> map in the Phase 2 RULING session (`R2`, UR-13); after `R2` it records each feature's ruling and is
> kept current.

**Owner:** the frontend lane (`docs/uplevel/README.md`, "Lanes"). **Created by:** `F2.2`
(`docs/uplevel/20-frontend.md`). **Traced at:** `main` `a890fd309`; `backend/` there is
byte-identical to the tree [`backend-entry-points.md`](backend-entry-points.md) traced, so its
cites hold here. Re-checked after merging `main` `eafc7148f`: of the files the rows cite, only
`backend/services/container_discovery.py` changed (`B1.6`), and F-140's two cites into it were
re-aimed. Re-checked again after merging `main` `9eb963ebf`: R47 (#6943) shifted every `backend/main.py` cite (re-aimed by diff) and wired the system broadcaster into the event emitter, which changed F-008's and F-016's evidence and §7's last row.

**Target Audiences:** the owner (R2), lane agents (Phase 3 deletions, Phase 4 feature packages),
reviewers.

---

## 1. How to read this document

### Statuses

The vocabulary is the README's (`docs/uplevel/README.md`, "Vocabulary"); no other status appears.

| status         | meaning here                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| -------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **unverified** | the chain exists end to end and the store it reads has a production writer (or the store it writes has a reader), but no golden path proves it yet. `F2.3` promotes it to **works** or demotes it to **half-built**.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| **half-built** | the chain breaks somewhere between the user and the store, and the evidence cites where by `path:line`: the route is missing or takes another verb (the client gets 404 or 405), the handler returns fixed or mock data, the store has no production writer, the handler reports success it did not achieve, a write has no reader — or the break is at the UI end: a control wired to nothing, or a built component no route renders. |
| **leftover**   | a surface for a capability that was retired (R8 and earlier: Florence-2, CLIP, X-CLIP, FashionCLIP, YOLO-World, pose and action recognition, the enrichment service, the Nemotron LLM, plate OCR); the evidence cites the retirement and what still shows it.                                                                                                                                                                          |

**A break at the UI end is half-built — a reading the owner is asked to confirm** (PR #6941, "Questions for the owner"; `R2` rules these rows either way). The README's examples of half-built are backend
faults, but the definition ("the surface exists, but … reports success it did not achieve") reads
the same from the other end. A button whose success toast covers a write that never happens, a
filter the list never applies, and a component built and wired to the API that no route renders are
each a feature the owner can **complete** or **retire** — which is what `R2` needs. Such a row says
"rendered by no route" in its surface, and its evidence cites the break.

### Row format (DECIDE)

One row per capability whose status could differ from its neighbours: a read-only page that works
is one row; an action that writes, or a panel whose data comes from a different chain, gets its own.

| column    | holds                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| --------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| id        | `F-nnn`, stable: never renumbered, never reused. New rows take the next free number.                                                                                                        |
| surface   | what a user would call it, then its route(s) in backticks (`/settings/cameras`); global chrome and external interfaces carry no route                                                       |
| action    | what the user does and sees                                                                                                                                                                 |
| API calls | each request as `` `METHOD /path` ``, `<br>`-separated; WebSockets as `` `WS /ws/…` ``. Parameter names are free: `{id}` and `{camera_id}` compare equal                                    |
| backend   | route handler at `path:line` → service → store (table, Redis key, file, outbound call)                                                                                                      |
| status    | **unverified**, **half-built** or **leftover**                                                                                                                                              |
| evidence  | why that status, with `path:line` cites: the production writer for **unverified**, the break for **half-built**, the retirement for **leftover**                                            |
| modules   | every module the feature uses, in every lane, as repo-relative paths. Phase 3 deletes a retired feature's modules from this list; Phase 4 consolidates a completed feature's tests from it. |
| real tier | the date the real tier last confirmed the row (`F2.3`); `—` until then                                                                                                                      |
| ruling    | **complete** or **retire**, from `R2`. Rows already ruled carry the ruling: inbound webhooks `complete (UR-12)`                                                                             |
| priority  | the owner's order among the completes, from `R2`                                                                                                                                            |

Each table is preceded by `<!-- prettier-ignore -->`, so editing one row never re-pads the others.

### The gate

`frontend/src/__tests__/feature-inventory.test.ts` checks this file against the code on every CI
run:

- every route `App.tsx` declares appears in some row's surface;
- every request a file reachable from `src/main.tsx` can send — as F1.1's endpoint-contract scanner
  (`frontend/scripts/api-contract-lib.mjs`) reads them — appears in some row's API calls, or in §5;
- every production file `src/main.tsx` cannot reach is claimed by a row or listed in §4, and §4
  lists no reachable file;
- every row has a status from the vocabulary and evidence (a `path:line` cite for **half-built**);
  every cited file exists and every cited line is inside it; every module exists;
- `frontend/knip.json` enters only through `src/main.tsx` and checks exports;
- every entry in F1.1's known-missing list names the row that owns it (or §5).

So a PR that adds a page, a request or a file without a row fails CI. **The gate's reach is the
scanner's.** It cannot see a request whose URL is held in a variable before `fetch`, composed onto a
base by a helper (`fetchAuditApi`, `fetchPromptApi`, `fetchModelZooApi`), built with `new URL`, or
opened through `buildWebSocketOptions`; the tracing for this file read those call sites by hand and
lists their requests in the rows, but the gate does not hold them there.

To read every cite beside the line it points at today, run
`node frontend/scripts/feature-inventory-cites.mjs` from the repo root. Line numbers drift as code
changes; a row whose cite no longer says what the evidence claims is re-traced, not re-numbered.

one component, it is one row carrying both routes.

## 4. Modules serving no feature

The modules no row claims. `R2` approves this list for deletion as a whole, naming any exceptions
(`docs/uplevel/templates/r2-sheet.md`, §3); `B3.2` and `F3.2` delete what it approves.

### Frontend

The 136 production files under `frontend/src/` that `src/main.tsx` cannot reach (37583 lines), minus the
files a row claims. The same set is `npx knip --production --include files` (run in `frontend/`)
with this PR's `knip.json`, and the gate's own import walk (`reachableFiles` in
`frontend/scripts/feature-inventory-lib.mjs`); the two agree file for file. Tests that test only
these files go with them (`F3.2`). **Last meaningful commit** is the newest commit touching the file
that changed fewer than 100 files, so squash merges and lockfile sweeps do not mask a file's age.
`00-audit.md` §4.3 counted 236 unreachable files at `d6ba78d5` by a method it does not record [A];
this list is measured at this head by the two methods above, which agree, and is the one to rule on.

## 5. Client requests no feature reaches

Requests in files `src/main.tsx` does reach, made only by code nothing mounts. Two kinds, each named
in **why**:

- an exported function no production module imports — knip's unused exports
  (`npx knip --production` in `frontend/`);
- a function whose every reference chain stops short of a route — checked with
  `node scripts/feature-inventory-callers.mjs <file> <function>` in `frontend/` (TypeScript
  `findReferences`, production files only) — or one a mounted hook builds but whose consumer never
  takes it (`startService`, `createHousehold`, `deleteHousehold`, read at the consumer's line).

They are dead code in live files, not features; `F3.2` removes them with the rest of the unreachable
code. F1.1's known-missing entry for `GET /api/prompts/history/{model}` points here: the function
that sends it, `fetchModelHistory`, is one of them.

## 6. The audit's seeds, re-verified

`00-audit.md` §10 classified eleven surfaces before this inventory existed. Each was traced again;
where the answer changed, the row's evidence says why.

<!-- prettier-ignore -->
| seed (`00-audit.md` §10) | seed status | rows | status now | what changed |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Model Zoo status card | works, partly | F-104, F-129 (cards); F-105, F-130 (latency chart) | unverified; half-built | split: the cards read the live model manager; the latency chart has no writer (`record_model_zoo_latency` has no caller) |
| Model load/unload panel (`ModelZooPanel`) | works | F-125 (read side), F-128 (load/unload) | unverified; half-built | load and unload always answer 501 (Triton runs `--model-control-mode=none`) |
| `/reid` dashboard and Re-ID matches panel | half-built | F-085, F-086, F-065 | half-built | confirmed; the `entities` table has no production writer since R8 S2b removed the enrichment pipeline |
| Prompt management page | half-built | F-120, F-122, F-123, F-124; F-121 | half-built; leftover | the four retired-model forms are split out as **leftover** |
| Automatic AI-audit evaluation (`BackgroundEvaluator`) | half-built | F-114 | half-built | confirmed |
| Camera anomaly timeline | half-built | F-210 | half-built | the cause changed: the route is now served (`F1.1`), but `get_recent_anomalies` returns `[]` unconditionally |
| Inbound webhooks | half-built, ruled complete | F-290 | half-built | confirmed; they now answer 501 (`B1.3`) |
| MQTT commands | half-built | F-291 | half-built | confirmed; `mqtt_command_handler.py` now has no importer at all (the seed named `inbound_webhooks.py`) |
| Action events panel | leftover | F-068 | leftover | confirmed |
| Pose skeleton overlay | leftover | F-069 | leftover | confirmed |
| Alert conditions: pose, action, threat, smoke/fire | leftover | F-220 | leftover | confirmed; the UI hard-codes threat and smoke/fire off and omits pose/action |

## 7. Breaks that span rows

Several half-built rows share one cause: a store the UI reads that nothing in production writes, or
a producer nothing calls. Fixing or retiring the cause settles every row it lists, which is how `R2`
can rule them.

<!-- prettier-ignore -->
| cause | evidence | rows |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Alert rules are never evaluated: `AlertRuleEngine.evaluate_event` and `create_alerts_for_event` have no production caller (the only construction is the test-rule dependency) | `backend/services/alert_engine.py:166`, `:1039`; `backend/api/dependencies.py:918` | F-216, F-217 |

## 8. Keeping this current

- **Adding a page, a request or a production file** fails the gate until a row (or §4/§5) accounts
  for it. Add the row in the same PR, with the next free id.
- **Changing a feature's chain** (a route moves, a writer appears or goes) updates its row's backend,
  evidence and modules in the same PR, under the README's cross-lane rule.
- **`F2.3`** sets **works** and the real-tier date per row, or demotes a row with its failing golden
  path as evidence; **`R2`'s record PR** fills ruling and priority (`R2a`'s recorded 2026-10-10 in #6980,
  ruling 89; `R2b`'s follows `F2.3`, ruling 86).
- **Phase 3** deletes a retired row's modules and then the row; the id is not reused.
- `scripts/feature-inventory-cites.mjs` prints each cite beside its current line;
  `scripts/feature-inventory-callers.mjs` prints the chain from a client function up to the route that
  mounts it, or says nothing mounts it (both in `frontend/`).
